Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 19, Step 4, "Any other person (other than your spouse) who lived with you all year as a member of your household if your relationship didn't violate local law"; i1040gi p. 19, Step 4, "or a son or daughter of any of them (for example, your niece or nephew)"; p501 p. 21, Multiple Support Agreement, "two or more persons, each of whom would be able to claim the person as a dependent but for the support test" (a qualifying-relative rule; the project refuses dependent.multiple_support only on its qualifying-relative test). executable-triage-v2 (rerun of triage.py's triage() with the witness cap lifted, record in scratch `/tmp/adj-bklg-dep-f2441/triage/dependent.deepseek.json`; 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,946 partial records from 40 bases; 9,011 comparisons): 8,560 of 9,011 agree; 168 candidate-unknown, 140 project-refuses, 75 value, 59 project-unknown, 9 candidate-refuses; on completed returns: 61 candidate-unknown, 11 value, 2 candidate-refuses. Its relationship test treats sibling_descendant (relatives more distant than a niece or nephew) as a relative who need not live with you, and its qualifying-child and qualifying-relative tests read dependent.joint_return, which the project asks only of a married person (37 completed unknowns). Every completed value (11, project yes and candidate no) is married no with joint_return yes, a record the interview cannot produce: dependent.married asks "married at the end of 2025 ..., or is a joint return being filed for 2025 that includes this person", so a No settles the joint return (classm.f8889.1 and .deepseek, classm.sli.3, mutation.t2.f8962_months_asset3, stage21.f1.lower_bound_still_decides, generated 20, 235, 436 (two entries), 445, 464). Its multiple-support refusal reaches qualifying children (21 completed). Project stands.

Second transcription's note: The form does not complete line 31 for a student whose American opportunity credit items are completed; the LLC amount is used only when the AOC path is not taken.

Ruling: model error; i8863 p. 3, Who cannot claim a credit, "5. The student has not been issued a TIN by the due date of his or her 2025 return (including extensions)"; p970 p. 11, "If an ATIN or ITIN is applied for on or before the due date of a 2025 return (including extensions) and the IRS issues an ATIN or ITIN as a result of the application, the IRS will consider the ATIN or ITIN as issued on or before the due date of the return"; i8863 p. 9, Line 31. executable-triage-v2 (rerun of triage.py's triage() with the witness cap lifted, record in scratch `/tmp/adj-bklg-dep-f2441/triage/dependent.deepseek.json`; 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,946 partial records from 40 bases; 9,011 comparisons): 4,874 of 9,011 agree; 4,123 candidate-unknown, 12 project-refuses, 2 value; on completed returns: 1,274 candidate-unknown. Its American opportunity test does not require the student to be claimed (it asks dependent.edu of every entry: 1,268 completed unknowns) and requires dependent.edu_tin_issued where the project accepts a timely application (6 completed unknowns); the 2 values are on refused returns. Project stands.

## Refusal gates listed by the second transcription

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

| line | gates listed | not read by the project's line |
| --- | --- | --- |
| dependent.claimed | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support | - |
| dependent.ctc_child | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support | dependent.multiple_support |
| dependent.odc_person | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support | - |
| dependent.ctc_amount | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support | dependent.multiple_support |
| dependent.odc_amount | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support | - |
| dependent.actc_amount | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support | dependent.multiple_support |
| dependent.hoh_test2 | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support | - |
| dependent.hoh_test1 | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support | - |
| dependent.eic_child | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support |
| dependent.eic_ssn_child | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support |
| dependent.dc_qp | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support | - |
| dependent.dc_count | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support |
| 2(d) | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support |

```yaml
lines:
  - id: dependent.dc_line2d
    printed: "2(d)"
    label: "Qualified expenses you incurred and paid in 2025 for the person listed in column (a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.dc_paid}, {guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}}, then: dependent.h_zero}]}
    note: "Chosen reading: line 2(d) is this person's qualified care expenses when the person is a qualifying person; otherwise zero. Benefits allocated on Form 2441 Part III are not reallocated per person here."
  - id: dependent.dc_line2c
    printed: "2(c)"
    label: "Check here if the qualifying person was over age 12 and was disabled."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [dependent.age, {const: 13, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_over12_care, {const: true}]}]}, {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [dependent.age, {const: 14, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}, {op: test, gt: [dependent.dc_paid, {const: 0}]}]}, {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {const: 13, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_under13, {const: false}]}, {op: test, gt: [dependent.dc_paid, {const: 0}]}]}]}
    note: "Chosen reading: a person age 14 or older at year end with disability and any care paid necessarily had that care while over age 12; age 13 can be determined only from the over-12 care input or from the absence of under-13 care."
  - id: dependent.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Don't enter more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc_eligible, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_aoc_expenses_cap}, {guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc_eligible, {const: true}]}}, then: dependent.h_zero}]}
    note: "Only an American opportunity credit eligible student completes this line; otherwise the amount is zero."
  - id: dependent.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.edu_line27, take: {const: 200000}}
  - id: dependent.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25% (0.25)."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: dependent.edu_line28, rate: {param: aoc.second_rate}}
  - id: dependent.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "If line 28 is zero, enter the amount from line 27. Otherwise, add $2,000 to the amount on line 29 and enter the result. Skip line 31."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line28, {const: 0}]}, then: dependent.edu_line27}, {guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line28, {const: 0}]}}, then: dependent.h_line30_add}]}
  - id: dependent.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Include the total of all amounts from all Parts III, line 31, on Part II, line 10."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc_eligible, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}, {guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc_eligible, {const: true}]}}, then: dependent.h_llc_amount}]}
    note: "The form does not complete line 31 for a student whose American opportunity credit items are completed; the LLC amount is used only when the AOC path is not taken."
  - id: dependent.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One dependent entry"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: dependent.one}
  - id: dependent.claimed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person is claimed as a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_dependent, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_dependent, {const: true}]}]}
    refuses: [dependent.married, dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support]
  - id: dependent.ctc_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person qualifies for the child tax credit"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_dependent, {const: true}]}, {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {const: 17, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.work_ssn, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [household.ctc_ssn, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: dependent.odc_person
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person qualifies for the credit for other dependents"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}, {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}}, {op: test, eq: [household.all_tin, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: dependent.ctc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Child tax credit amount attributable to this person"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_ctc_per_child}, {guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}}, then: dependent.h_zero}]}
  - id: dependent.odc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Credit for other dependents amount attributable to this person"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_odc_per_dependent}, {guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: true}]}}, then: dependent.h_zero}]}
  - id: dependent.actc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional child tax credit amount attributable to this person"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_actc_per_child}, {guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}}, then: dependent.h_zero}]}
  - id: dependent.hoh_absence
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "An unreasonable absence was counted in time lived with you"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: dependent.absence_no_return}
  - id: dependent.hoh_test2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person counts for the head of household test based on living in the home more than half the year"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.h_hoh_person, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_hoh_residence, {const: true}]}, {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.hoh_absence, {const: true}]}}, {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_hoh_excluded, {const: true}]}}]}
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support]
  - id: dependent.hoh_test1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This parent counts for the head of household test based on the parent's main home"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.parent_home_paid, {const: true}]}]}
    refuses: [dependent.multiple_support]
  - id: dependent.eic_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person is a qualifying child for the earned income credit"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_relationship, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_age, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.us_half, {const: true}]}, {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.married, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}]}]}
    refuses: [dependent.married, dependent.divorced_rule]
  - id: dependent.eic_ssn_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person is an EIC qualifying child with a valid EIC social security number"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: dependent.dc_qp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person is a qualifying person for child and dependent care expenses"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, all: [{op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {const: 13, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}]}, {op: test, all: [{op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {const: 14, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_under13, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}]}, {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}, {op: test, gt: [dependent.months, {const: 6, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}]}]}
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule]
  - id: dependent.dc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Count of this person as a qualifying person for child and dependent care"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: percent_floor, part: dependent.h_dc_count_money, whole: {const: 100}, cap: 1}
    note: "Read as 1 when this person is a qualifying person for dependent care and 0 otherwise, represented through percent_floor over a one-cent amount so the declared Count type is preserved."
  - id: dependent.ptc_magi_counted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This dependent's modified AGI counted on Form 8962 line 2b"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.ptc_magi}, {guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}}, then: dependent.h_zero}]}
  - id: dependent.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero money amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Child tax credit amount per qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ctc.per_child}
  - id: dependent.h_odc_per_dependent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Credit for other dependents amount per dependent"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: odc.per_dependent}
  - id: dependent.h_actc_per_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional child tax credit amount per qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: actc.per_child}
  - id: dependent.h_aoc_expense_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "American opportunity credit expense cap"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.expense_cap}
  - id: dependent.h_aoc_full_band
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "American opportunity credit full-band amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.full_band}
  - id: dependent.h_edu_adj_add
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total education expense adjustments"
    type: NonNegDollars

  - id: dependent.h_edu_aoc_gross
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "American opportunity credit qualified expenses before adjustments"
    type: NonNegDollars

  - id: dependent.h_edu_adj_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"

    label: "Lifetime learning credit adjusted qualified education expenses"

    printed: "(helper)"

    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [dependent.h_edu_adj_aoc, dependent.h_aoc_expense_cap]}

    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This student meets the American opportunity credit student requirements"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_ein, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_tin_issued, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line23, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line24, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line25, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line26, {const: false}]}]}
  - id: dependent.h_llc_allowed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This student has expenses eligible for the lifetime learning credit"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_tin_issued, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: dependent.h_llc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lifetime learning credit adjusted expenses when allowed"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_llc_allowed, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_edu_adj_llc}, {guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_llc_allowed, {const: true}]}}, then: dependent.h_zero}]}
  - id: dependent.h_line30_add
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 29 plus the $2,000 American opportunity credit base"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_line29, dependent.h_aoc_full_band]}
  - id: dependent.h_qc_relationship
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Relationship test for a qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qc_age
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Age test for a qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, all: [{op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {const: 19, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}]}, {op: test, all: [{op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {const: 24, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.student, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.disabled, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qc_residence
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Residency test for a qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, gt: [dependent.months, {const: 6, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.kidnapped, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qc_support
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Support test for a qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [dependent.self_support, {const: false}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qc_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Joint return test for a qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qc_citizen
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Citizen or Canada/Mexico residence test"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.canada_mexico, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qc_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "No disqualifying other qualifying-child claim"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_nontaxpayer, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: dependent.h_taxpayer_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The taxpayer is not barred from claiming dependents by being claimable as a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qc_dependent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person is a qualifying child claimed as a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_relationship, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_age, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_support, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_joint, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_residence, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_citizen, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_other, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_taxpayer_ok, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qr_relationship
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Relationship or member-of-household test for a qualifying relative"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent_relative"}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "in_law"}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.spouse_relative, {const: true}]}, {op: test, all: [{op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "household_member"}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "other"}]}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.qr_all_year, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.lawful, {const: true}]}]}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qr_not_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person is not a qualifying child of the taxpayer or another taxpayer"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_dependent, {const: true}]}}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_other, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qr_gross
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Gross income test for a qualifying relative"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [dependent.gross_income, {param: qr.gross_income_limit}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qr_support
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Support test for a qualifying relative"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [dependent.supported, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.multiple_support]
  - id: dependent.h_qr_citizen
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Citizen or Canada/Mexico residence test for a qualifying relative"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.canada_mexico, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qr_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Joint return test for a qualifying relative"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qr_dependent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person is a qualifying relative claimed as a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_relationship, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_not_qc, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_gross, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_support, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_citizen, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_joint, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_taxpayer_ok, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: dependent.h_hoh_person
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person is claimed or is a qualifying child for head of household purposes"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_dependent, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: dependent.h_hoh_excluded
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person is a dependent only because the person lived with you all year"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "household_member"}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "other"}]}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.qr_all_year, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: dependent.h_hoh_residence
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person lived in the home more than half the year for head of household purposes"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, gt: [dependent.months, {const: 6, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [dependent.kidnapped, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: dependent.h_dc_one_cent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One cent for representing a dependent-care count"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sc.one_cent}
  - id: dependent.h_dc_count_money
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One cent when this person is a dependent-care qualifying person, otherwise zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_dc_one_cent}, {guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}}, then: dependent.h_zero}]}
```
